# Reciprocal History and the RH Route

## SLC-GEN3-R4: current computation — 16 September 2026

SLC-GEN3-R4 and SLC-GEN3-CEV1-R4 are the sole current global runtime and CE, generation GEN3-SOURCEOPERATORS1-20260916-G1 (H001469). Complete exact histories and logarithmic accounts now include certified scalar/spectral logs and eight source-operator operations. Source-operator qualification records 50 candidate and 157 installed-adoption checks. The public portable package retains its September10 R3 binding; the current R4 capability sources and research records are a separate supplement. GEN3-RXT-R7.1 retains its September14 deployment context.

[Current derivations, code and results](../../docs/ARCHITECTURE.md).

## Retained source-era derivation and results

The following development retains its original experimental context and revision fields. Historical engine selections and campaign status in this source-era account are superseded by the dated current section above.


## Conceptual abstract

Reciprocal History begins with paths, not scalar state counts. On the unchanged
N100 graph, every undirected edge has two orientations. The resulting 520
directed-edge states support a nonbacktracking transfer operator that records
ordered continuation, reversal and primitive-cycle data. Pairing 10,700
oriented primitives with their reverses produces 5,350 reciprocal classes
with exact carrier-weighted norms and power laws. This is not the N100 DOS
renamed: the DOS counts assignments by energy, while RH retains ordered
history on the same source grammar.

The stage chain contains a decisive preserved failure. P2 direct asked whether
the history object itself supplied the ordinary-prime Euler coefficient. Its
first coefficient was 100 rather than 2, so that direct identification is
falsified at its exact scope. P2A and P2B are successors, not retrospective
relabels. P2A reconstructs ordinary primes through the radix-12 first-
singularity tower \(n=\operatorname{ord}_q(12)\). P2B supplies coefficient one
from the N100 role/depth grading, while N95 and N105 controls return index zero.
No nontrivial-zero ordinate chooses the tower.

The accumulation field supplies the real coordinate that turns multiplication
into translation:

\[
q=\frac{A_{total}-A_0}{1-A_0}=\frac{r_c}{r},
\qquad
u=-\log q=\log\frac r{r_c}.
\]

Scaling \(r\mapsto nr\) becomes \(u\mapsto u+\log n\), and the half-density
becomes \(n^{-1/2}\). On this coordinate the operator
\(L=-d^2/du^2+1/4\) has endpoint null modes \(e^{\pm u/2}\), giving the domain
on which the completed prime-power translations act.

P3 retains the completed arithmetic

\[
\xi(s)=\tfrac12s(s-1)\pi^{-s/2}\Gamma(s/2)\zeta(s)=\xi(1-s)
\]

and the paired completed Weil form. Its continuous energy, finite centered
von-Mangoldt displacement sum and floor term form one object; splitting them
into individually divergent pieces changes the calculation.

Four authority surfaces remain distinct: the public RH claim, the internal
universal-nonnegativity assignment, optional finite F81 spectral research, and
operational SLC/Starbreaker integration. The current operational handle is
`SLCQ2-RZ-RH-MOMENT-ADAPTER-V1`. It does not silently merge those questions.

## 1. Opening question and conceptual picture

The governing question is:

> How does a finite source grammar carry reciprocal path information into an
> ordinary-prime tower, a real logarithmic translation coordinate and the
> completed arithmetic form while preserving the exact failure and authority
> boundaries encountered along the way?

The chain is

```text
N100 source graph
  -> 520 directed nonbacktracking states
  -> primitive histories and reciprocal classes
  -> P2 direct obstruction retained
  -> P2A radix-12 prime tower + P2B coefficient-one grading
  -> accumulation-derived u = log(r/r_c)
  -> pi/Gamma completion and xi reciprocity
  -> completed Weil quadratic form
  -> separately typed public, internal, spectral and operational surfaces
```

No successor is allowed to erase the failed direct identification. Each new
arrow supplies a different missing structure.

## 2. The reciprocal-history object

### 2.1 Directed-edge state space

N100 has 260 undirected source edges. Orient each edge both ways. The directed
state census is therefore

\[
2\cdot260=520.
\]

Write a directed edge as \(e=(u,v)\). The nonbacktracking transfer \(B\) has
entries

\[
B_{(u,v),(v,w)}=
\begin{cases}
1,&w\ne u,\\
0,&w=u.
\end{cases}
\]

Thus a step may continue through \(v\) but may not immediately undo the edge
just traversed. Powers \(B^n\) count ordered nonbacktracking histories of
length \(n\), and traces \(\operatorname{tr}(B^n)\) retain closed directed
history. Primitive-cycle and determinant receipts separate repeated powers
from primitive orbit data.

### 2.2 Reciprocal pairing

Reversal sends

\[
(v_0,v_1,\ldots,v_n)
\longmapsto
(v_n,v_{n-1},\ldots,v_0).
\]

P1A3/P1B retain 10,700 oriented primitives. Pairing each history with its
reverse gives

\[
\frac{10{,}700}{2}=5{,}350
\]

reciprocal classes. The paired object retains the common contribution and the
directional difference, with integer carrier-weighted norms and exact power
laws.

### 2.3 Why RH is not DOS

The N100 DOS is

\[
g(E)=\#\{\sigma:H(\sigma)=E\}.
\]

The reciprocal-history transfer is indexed by directed edges and ordered
continuations. Two histories may end at the same state and contribute to the
same DOS sector while remaining distinct under reversal, primitive length or
ordered path. Conversely, a DOS coefficient contains no data sufficient to
reconstruct an oriented primitive. The two objects share source provenance;
they do not share type.

## 3. Executed stage chain and deviation

### 3.1 P0 through P1B

The foundation is cumulative:

- P0 builds the exact 520-state nonbacktracking transfer and retains traces,
  reversal, primitives and determinant receipts.
- P1A compiles tensor and Euler types.
- P1A2 keeps W9 as five derived non-counted receipts.
- P1A3/P1B pair the 10,700 oriented primitives into 5,350 reciprocal classes
  with native norms.

The five W9 receipts remain outputs of completion. They do not increase the
native source-row census.

### 3.2 Preserved P2 direct failure

P2 direct tested the simplest possible bridge: identify the history-prime
Euler coefficient directly with the ordinary-prime coefficient. The first
history coefficient is 100, while the target ordinary-prime coefficient is 2.
Because

\[
100\ne2,
\]

the direct identity is falsified at that scope. The mismatch is structural,
not a tolerance or serialization fault.

For this exact direct-identification test, the registered classification is:
**The test falsifies the concept.**

The correct record is

```text
P2 direct question
  -> first exact coefficient 100
  -> required direct coefficient 2
  -> direct identification falsified
  -> obstruction retained
```

Neither P2A nor P2B changes these two numbers.

### 3.3 P2A successor: radix-12 first singularity

For an ordinary prime \(q\nmid12\), 12 is a unit modulo \(q\). Its powers form
a finite subgroup of \((\mathbb Z/q\mathbb Z)^\times\), so there is a least
positive integer

\[
n=\operatorname{ord}_q(12)
\]

with

\[
12^n\equiv1\pmod q.
\]

Minimality places \(q\) in the first cyclotomic singularity:

\[
q\mid\Phi_n(12)
\]

subject to the exact prime-power/order bookkeeping of the tower. P2A uses
this first block order to reconstruct ordinary-prime placement. It adds a
radix-12 arithmetic route that P2 direct lacked.

### 3.4 P2B successor: source grading

P2B uses the N100 role/depth grading

\[
C_0|C_1|C_2=20|24|5,
\]

\[
H_0|H_1|H_2=8|7|0
\]

to supply the compiled family index 1. The source-native N95 and N105 controls
return index 0. This distinguishes the selected N100 grading from nearby row-
count perturbations without consulting any nontrivial-zero ordinate.

The successor logic is

```text
P2A: ordinary prime q -> first radix-12 order ord_q(12)
P2B: N100 role/depth grading -> coefficient one
controls: N95 and N105 -> zero
```

## 4. Accumulation to logarithmic translation

### 4.1 Field coordinate

The accumulation relation is

\[
q=\frac{A_{total}-A_0}{1-A_0}=\frac{r_c}{r}.
\]

Taking the negative logarithm gives

\[
u=-\log q
=-\log\frac{r_c}{r}
=\log\frac r{r_c}.
\]

If the radial ratio is multiplied by \(n>0\),

\[
r\mapsto nr
\quad\Longrightarrow\quad
u\mapsto\log\frac{nr}{r_c}=u+\log n.
\]

Multiplicative arithmetic has become additive translation.

### 4.2 Half-density

The symmetric translation difference acts at \(\pm t/2\). For
\(t=\log n\), the decaying half-density factor is

\[
e^{-t/2}=e^{-\log(n)/2}=n^{-1/2}.
\]

This derives the prime-power weight from the logarithmic displacement rather
than inserting it as a separate numerical convention.

### 4.3 Endpoint operator

Define

\[
L=-\frac{d^2}{du^2}+\frac14.
\]

For \(f_\pm(u)=e^{\pm u/2}\),

\[
f_\pm''=\frac14f_\pm,
\]

so

\[
Lf_\pm=-\frac14f_\pm+\frac14f_\pm=0.
\]

The two endpoint null modes generate the two-moment boundary. The declared
test domain is

\[
\mathcal D_0=L(C_c^\infty(\mathbb R)),
\]

which keeps compact support and endpoint annihilation visible.

Accumulation supplies this coordinate route. It is not identified with the
N100 DOS, a local P5A symbol or a numerical energy coefficient.

## 5. Completed arithmetic

### 5.1 Lambda and xi

Start from

\[
\Lambda(s)=\pi^{-s/2}\Gamma(s/2)\zeta(s).
\]

The completed entire function is

\[
\xi(s)=\frac12s(s-1)\Lambda(s).
\]

The π/Gamma factor supplies the archimedean completion, while \(s(s-1)\)
handles the pole bookkeeping. The resulting reciprocity is

\[
\xi(s)=\xi(1-s).
\]

Prime powers enter through the von Mangoldt weight \(\Lambda(n)\), not only
through primes at exponent one.

### 5.2 Translation energy

Let

\[
(T_ah)(u)=h(u+a).
\]

Define the reciprocal difference

\[
B_th=T_{t/2}h-T_{-t/2}h
\]

and its nonnegative route energy

\[
E_t(h)=\|B_th\|^2.
\]

The construction is direction-sensitive before the norm and reciprocal after
pairing.

### 5.3 Completed Weil form

The compiled form is

\[
Q_\xi(h)=
\int_0^\infty
\frac{e^{-t/2}}{1-e^{-2t}}E_t(h)\,dt
+\sum_{n\ge2}\frac{\Lambda(n)}{\sqrt n}
\left[E_{\log n}(h)-2\|h\|^2\right]
-\kappa_0\|h\|^2,
\]

where

\[
\kappa_0
=\gamma-\log(3A_0)+\frac\pi2+\log2.
\]

For compactly supported \(h\), \(T_{t/2}h\) and \(T_{-t/2}h\) have disjoint
support once \(t\) exceeds the support diameter. Then

\[
E_t(h)=2\|h\|^2,
\]

so the centered summand vanishes. The prime-power sum is therefore finite on
each compactly supported input.

The continuous route-energy integral, centered arithmetic sum and floor
subtraction are one completed object. Separating them into divergent pieces
would change the domain and value of the form.

For positive reels, the exact native law is

b\!\left(\sum_iG_i\right)\geq\sum_i b(G_i),

with equality controlled by common minimizers and strictness by kernel

removes arbitrary transport and identifies the intrinsic current

Each admitted prime-power event separates into an exact linear debit and a

z^*(K_{bg}'+R_k')z>4w.

Here `P_C4^4=I` while cumulative wave action has positive derivative. The

These controls preserve rather than weaken the positive completed arithmetic
already in custody.

# Starbreaker Formation Engine

## Starbreaker: signed history and native construction — 14 September 2026

SB-GEN3-ACCUMULATION-R1 is installed on SLC-GEN3-R3; SB-GEN3-RXT-R2 is its joint native successor. The engine retains signed J4 amplitudes, exact source action, ordered logarithmic accumulation and 45 acquired construction policies. All 768 checked closed cycles retain positive variation and zero net change. The common receiver recovers all 98,304 tested orders and barriers. The test result suggests strong contact with the concept. Moving-body source, fixed-site encounter geometry and physical propagation/readout remain the next construction.

[Current derivations, code and results](../../research/starbreaker/README.md).

## Retained source-era derivation and results

The following development retains its original experimental context and revision fields. Historical engine selections and campaign status in this source-era account are superseded by the dated current section above.


## Conceptual abstract

Starbreaker is a formation application with its own native grammar. It retains
2,976 ledgers, 482,112 typed atoms, seed/collapse/final stages, two event
orders, two D18 pair maps, same and reversed occurrence views, contact
incidence, record origin and constrained M126/N144/N144+d/L162 readouts. N100
contributes structure-first routing discipline; its 100-spin graph is not
substituted for this application.

The exact state ladder begins with M126 retention. Fee-exempt Theta18 provides
an independent 18-coordinate lift, so \(2^{126}\cdot2^{18}=2^{144}\). L162 is
an ambient occurrence view whose valid image still contains \(2^{144}\)
states, not \(2^{162}\) independent matter configurations. Active mismatch
coordinates travel in an N144+d sidecar rather than being promoted to another
object or capacity.

The structure-first replay detects those native quotient, fiber and readout
certificates. It reproduces the completed parent byte for byte, passes 78/78
checks and never invokes dense H14F fallback. The W9 history refinement adds a
rank-161 target-local order-history receipt, but the mode is emit-only: it may
record cross-stage memory and may not alter scheduler grouping or allocation
without a separate tested promotion.

The Courtroom evidence supplies a complete methodology chain. CR210 reconstructs
a scoped external-collapse directional-contact surface. CR211 freezes a typed
162-slot particle-ledger interface while leaving address-to-template incidence
null. CR005c identifies why stage ordering cannot be promoted to physical
escape time. CR005d builds a dimensionless continuous flow and carrier-QNM
source bridge. CR005e stops on a numerical trace-residue gate; CR005f freezes
one exact trace-free correction; CR005g runs the unchanged morphology design
and records the boundary that no stable Starbreaker/CCSN morphology match
cleared the gates. CR005h constructs a complete per-carrier ledger but fails
three controls; CR005i corrects only those controls, independently replays all
96 scenarios and 53,568 carriers, adopts the byte-identical ledger and leaves
CR005h immutable.

## 1. Opening question and conceptual picture

The governing question is:

> How does Starbreaker carry a source-native formation history from typed
> ledgers through exact state accounting, directional contact, continuous
> source construction and controlled application readouts without replacing
> its grammar, erasing failed tests or promoting dimensionless receipts into
> physical observables?

The application picture is

```text
Starbreaker ledgers and typed atoms
  -> seed / collapse / final formation stages
  -> lexicographic and reverse event orders
  -> exact M126 retention
  -> fee-exempt Theta18 lift to N144
  -> optional active mismatch sidecar N144+d
  -> constrained L162 occurrence views
  -> emit-only W9 rank-161 history
  -> dimensionless source/application bridges
  -> physical calibration joins remain explicit
```

The route is layered. A state count is not a waveform. A source quadrupole is
not strain. A carrier with positive source influence is not the substance
transported by a gravitational wave. Keeping those types separate is what
allows the exact and exploratory work to accumulate safely.

## 2. Native objects, counts and type boundaries

### 2.1 Ledger and atom census

Each of 2,976 ledgers carries one 162-slot typed surface, giving

\[
2{,}976\cdot162=482{,}112
\]

structured atoms. The slots are not assumed occupied merely because they
exist. Unary, pair, triad, carrier, support, scalar and rejected classes remain
distinct, and categorical relations remain unresolved until their own geometry
stage.

Starbreaker has three stages and two event orders. Therefore the number of
stage/order snapshots is

\[
2{,}976\cdot3\cdot2=17{,}856.
\]

Executing both D18 pair-map controls doubles the runtime rows:

\[
17{,}856\cdot2=35{,}712.
\]

These identities are direct state-accounting checks. A dropped ledger, stage,
order or pair map changes the total immediately.

### 2.2 Persistent and transient types

The formation engine preserves the following firewall:

| Type | Role | Nonidentity |
|---|---|---|
| \(O\) | persistent matter identity | not a substrate site phase |
| W8 | unresolved/returned substrate-site phase | not a retained history rank |
| W9 | direct target phase during active contact | not an independent source row |
| X1 | one transient H2 mirror bit | not matter and ledger contribution zero |
| U | coordinated reciprocal record exchange | not a new material object |
| M126 | retained matter/output state | not universal occurrence transport |
| Theta18 | fee-exempt directional carrier/fiber | not retained mass lift |
| N144+d | active defect-sidecar custody | not extra independent capacity |
| L162 | constrained occurrence readout | not \(2^{162}\) independent physical states |

Completion returns both contacted sites to W8 and clears X1 while retaining
updated records. “State returns; history does not” means the endpoint type can
repeat while its reciprocal receipt remains nonzero.

### 2.3 Stage and control surfaces

The two event orders are lexicographic and reverse-lexicographic. The retained
pair-map controls are `D18_P1_SHIFT_14` and `D18_M1_SHIFT_08`. Same and reversed
views test occurrence reconstruction from opposite readout orientations.

These are application fixtures. A pair map or contact-incidence field does not
become a physical matter mirror, q orientation, Theta partition or elementwise
pairing unless a separate source selects it.

## 3. Construction interfaces

### 3.2 Continuous-flow and carrier-QNM bridge

[`CR:CR005d@21`](../tests/courtroom/21-gravitational-waves-cr005d-starbreaker-continuous-flow-and-carrier-qnm-source-bridge/README.md) replaces the earlier affine stage probe with a zero-fit
continuous structural flow. The three stored states are joined by exponential
radial collapse, a bounce impulse at dimensionless phase \(t=1\), and constant
radial plus shortest-branch angular release. The maximum reconstruction error
at the stored states is \(2.191\times10^{-14}\).

For unit carrier positions \(x_c(t)\), a trace-free quadrupole has the form

\[
Q_{ab}(t)=\sum_c\left[x_{c,a}(t)x_{c,b}(t)
-\frac{\delta_{ab}}{3}|x_c(t)|^2\right].
\]

The source surface uses \(d^2Q_{ab}/dt^2\); a third derivative supplies a
power proxy. The carrier-QNM response convolves the source with the sealed

\[
g(u)=\frac{e^{-(4/45)u}\sin((3/8)u)}{3/8},
\]

so \(\omega_RM=3/8\) and \(\omega_IM=4/45\) are fixed rather than fitted.

The run tracks 1,993 dual carriers. Of these, 1,541 satisfy escape before

\[
\frac{1{,}541}{1{,}993}=0.7732062217762168.
\]

The median closure-minus-escape margin is 0.04020605301428759. Across the
construction there are 10,515,538 transition roots and zero root failures.
All 14/14 construction gates and 10/10 controls pass; all 96 scenarios and
482,112 atoms are present. Maximum relative trace error is
\(2.5997906629060057\times10^{-15}\).

The bridge remains dimensionless. Unit carrier weights are not matter masses,

## 4. Exact formation-state derivation

### 4.1 M126 retention

The structured application begins with two exact N72 custody packets. Each

\[
2^{72}=4{,}722{,}366{,}482{,}869{,}645{,}213{,}696
\]

binary states as a \(64\times10\) conditional tensor. Their declared matter

\[
2^{126}
=85{,}070{,}591{,}730{,}234{,}615{,}865{,}843{,}651{,}857{,}942{,}052{,}864
\]

M126 configurations across 4,096 two-sided sectors. The scalar background has
127 oriented marked sectors and 64 occupied energy bins from \(-7875\) through
63.

M126 retains record origin without promotion in 468 stage/order snapshots and
requires promotion in 17,388. Those counts sum to the complete snapshot census:

\[
468+17{,}388=17{,}856.
\]

The split demonstrates why M126 is a retention/output state, not a universal
occurrence-transport state.

### 4.2 Fee-exempt Theta18 lift

Theta18 contributes 18 independent directional coordinates, so

\[
2^{126}\cdot2^{18}=2^{144}
\]

and

\[
2^{144}
=22{,}300{,}745{,}198{,}530{,}623{,}141{,}535{,}718{,}272{,}648{,}361{,}505{,}980{,}416.
\]

The lift is fee-exempt: no Theta multiplicity is folded into X1 and no retained
mass is attached to the carrier fiber.

### 4.3 N144+d and L162 readout

For a same-view mismatch mask, define

\[
d_t=\operatorname{popcount}(m_t),\qquad0\le d_t\le18.
\]

The application route is

```text
d = 0       -> exact N144 independent union state
0 < d < 18  -> N144 plus active d-coordinate sidecar
d = 18      -> L162 constrained occurrence readout
```

The L162 ambient ceiling is \(2^{162}\), but each valid same or reversed image
contains \(2^{144}\) states. Their ratio is

\[
\frac{2^{162}}{2^{144}}=2^{18}=262{,}144.
\]

Thus L162 supplies 18 constrained occurrence coordinates over the N144 image;
it does not add 18 independent matter spins.

### 4.4 Byte-identical structure-first replay

The v1.0 replay inspects Starbreaker's own certificates and chooses the M126
quotient, N144 fiber lift, N144+d sidecar and L162 constrained readout. Dense
H14F fallback is unnecessary. The exact route census is

| Output route | Rows |
|---|---:|
| N144 | 1,262 |
| N144+d | 17,922 |
| total non-extreme | 19,184 |

The replay retains all 2,976 ledgers, 20,415,176 onset events per order and
35,712 runtime rows. It passes 78/78 checks in 5.251708 seconds, and fresh
runtime output is byte-identical to the completed parent. The method changes;
the Starbreaker grammar and output do not.

### 4.5 Emit-only W9 history

The target-local W9 history operator is present across discovery/validation,
both pair maps and both endpoint gauges. Every branch has cumulative and
cross-stage rank 161. Seed equals its stage-local control; collapse and final
retain nonzero cross-stage memory.

`W9HistoryMode` is emit-only. It may return the typed history sidecar but has no
authority to change phase-pool grouping, N144 allocation, N144+d fallback or
L162 routing. A scheduling use would require a separate test and promotion.

## 5. Focused application evidence

### 5.1 External-collapse directional-contact replay

[`CR:CR210@15`](../tests/courtroom/15-scale-bridge-simulator-cr210-starbreaker-3d-external-collapse-contact/README.md) regenerated all 1,218 three-dimensional simulations, rebuilt the
no-fit external contact from fresh outputs and passed all 58 Starbreaker
regression tests. All 28 precommitted source/output files reproduced their
SHA-256 digests.

Both remnant ladders remain strictly ordered across \(p=0,1,2,3\). All 42
individual ladders rise at every step, all 42 inward \(p=3\) allocations exceed
their same-total uniform controls, and both aligned allocations are maximal
among all 24 radial permutations.

The source verdict is a scoped qualitative directional-contact pass. It does
not identify \(p\) with physical compactness, identify a remnant with a black
hole, test faster formation time, solve hydrodynamics or relativity, calibrate
A or promote the packing law physically.

### 5.2 Inventory-corrected pixel-bridge retest

[`CR:CR005g@21`](../tests/courtroom/21-gravitational-waves-cr005g-starbreaker-weak-ccsn-pixel-bridge-inventory-corrected/README.md) executes the morphology question frozen in CR005e and corrected
in CR005f. Ten public three-dimensional weak-CCSN matter-strain waveforms are
compared with all 96 Starbreaker post-bounce unit-carrier quadrupole sources
across 13 observer directions. Each source becomes the same normalized
\(48\times64\) time-frequency power grid.

For normalized pixel distributions \(P,Q\), the primary statistic is

\[
\mathcal A(P,Q)=\sum_{pixel}\sqrt{P_{pixel}Q_{pixel}}.
\]

The best-template search is replayed inside 47 time-shift nulls and the locked
reversal controls. All 12/12 construction gates and 11/11 controls pass. The
door results are

| Door | Observed median | Shift q90 | Shift q95 | Clears gate |
|---|---:|---:|---:|---|
| discovery | 0.150857 | 0.155669 | 0.155861 | no |
| validation | 0.068025 | 0.094694 | 0.096175 | no |
| final | 0.076474 | 0.073062 | 0.073193 | not q95/all |

The largest individual affinity is 0.407647 for
`s9.0.swbj15.horo.3d.gw.dat` against Starbreaker `REF_0272` in view
`face_yz_m`. Because the precommitted stable-door gates do not close, the
source result is

```text
BOUNDARY_PIXEL_SURFACE_CONSTRUCTED__NO_STABLE_STARBREAKER_CCSN_MORPHOLOGY_MATCH
```

This is normalized morphology only, not amplitude or mechanism identity.

### 5.3 Independent carrier-ledger replay and freeze

[`CR:CR005i@21`](../tests/courtroom/21-gravitational-waves-cr005i-starbreaker-per-carrier-gw-control-appeal-and-freeze/README.md) independently replays all 96 scenarios and all 53,568 carrier
rows under the corrected controls. The parent authority matches exactly. The
maximum discrete-linearity observed/bound ratio is \(5.253916\times10^{-3}\),
and the maximum integrated-power ordering difference is
\(1.616856\times10^{-9}\), below the frozen \(10^{-6}\) limit. Static
quadrupole residual and derivative are zero. Isotropic cancellation residual
is \(1.370646\times10^{-16}\).

The byte-identical CR005h carrier ledger is adopted and frozen. Seven slots
are strong source-contributor candidates—16, 13, 14, 15, 4, 2 and 3—and slot
17 is directional. Positive leave-one-out influence means reinforcement of
the internal source proxy. It does not mean that a carrier is transported GW
material.

## 6. Preserved deviation and correction chains

### 6.1 Preserved CR005e numerical stop

`CR:CR005e@SAM-RESEARCH` freezes the external morphology design before
execution: ten Princeton three-dimensional weak-CCSN waveforms, 96 Starbreaker
scenarios, 252 carriers per scenario, 13 directions, 2,049 samples, three
trimmed derivative-edge samples, a \(48\times64\) pixel grid, Hellinger
affinity, same-door matching, 47 shifts and reversal controls.

The first Starbreaker scenario stopped because independently finite-differenced
diagonal tensor components retained relative trace residue

\[
9.43617769285563\times10^{-9},
\]

above the frozen \(10^{-10}\) gate. No match, null distribution, door statistic
or scientific verdict opened. CR005e remains immutable.

### 6.2 Trace-free projection correction

`CR:CR005f@SAM-RESEARCH` freezes one permitted correction. Trace freedom
requires

\[
S_{xx}+S_{yy}+S_{zz}=0.
\]

After the two locked finite differences, the successor sets

\[
S_{zz}:=-(S_{xx}+S_{yy})
\]

before observer projection. This changes no source, door, sample count, grid,
view, statistic, null, evidence ladder or physical boundary. It is an exact
algebraic projection with zero fitted parameters, not a relaxed threshold.
CR005f is the correction precommit; the executed retest is CR005g.

### 6.3 Per-carrier ledger control failure

[`CR:CR005h@21`](../tests/courtroom/21-gravitational-waves-cr005h-starbreaker-per-carrier-gw-candidate-ledger/README.md) constructs a complete 53,568-row per-carrier attribution
ledger. Every occurrence receives a unit-occurrence trace-free quadrupole,
individual power, exact leave-one-out aggregate-power change, bind/escape
class and frozen identity. Positive

\[
\Delta P=P_{aggregate}-P_{without\ carrier}
\]

marks reinforcement; negative \(\Delta P\) marks coherent cancellation.

The candidate content was complete, but exactly three construction controls
failed, so the campaign verdict remains

```text
FAIL_PER_CARRIER_GW_LEDGER_CONSTRUCTION
```

The ledger is preserved rather than deleted or tuned to pass.

### 6.4 Control-appeal correction

CR005i precommits three implementation corrections:

1. G02 compares the sealed CR005d verdict against its exact string instead of
   the wrong shortened prefix.
2. G05 evaluates third-difference linearity inside a frozen roundoff envelope
   and retains an independent integrated-power guard, rather than demanding
   literal floating equality after derivative amplification.
3. G08 first checks static constancy and isotropic cancellation at the
   quadrupole level, then bounds derivative roundoff, rather than demanding a
   literal post-third-difference zero.

The envelope is fixed before the appeal runner:

\[
512\,\epsilon_{mach}\,\|D^3\|_\infty
\max_t\sum_i|Q_i(t)|.
\]

Candidate scores, thresholds, histories, flow and every ledger row remain
unchanged. The correction step and the independent replay in §5.3 are separate
links. CR005h remains the immutable failed parent after adoption.

The two cumulative chains are

```text
CR005e failure -> CR005f correction precommit -> CR005g executed retest
CR005h control failure -> CR005i bounded correction -> CR005i independent replay/freeze
```

## 7. Observability and physical boundary

### 7.1 Escape and closure observability boundary

[`CR:CR005c@21`](../tests/courtroom/21-gravitational-waves-cr005c-starbreaker-escape-closure-observability-gate/README.md) begins with the frozen three-stage history grammar:

```text
101 -> contact, open, returned contact
010 -> open, transient contact, open
100 -> contact, open, open
110 -> contact, contact, open
```

There are 8,340 history-101 relations and 59,191,245 ending-zero relations.
For every 101 relation, a piecewise-affine Cartesian probe finds one outward
crossing on seed-to-collapse and one inward crossing on collapse-to-final.
Median coordinates are

\[
\lambda_{release}=0.566282338,
\]

\[
\lambda_{return}=1.132859211,
\]

with open interval 0.617486748.

These values establish local near-far-near geometry. They do not establish
physical timing because \(\lambda\) is a stage-normalized affine coordinate
and the order \(\lambda_{release}<\lambda_{return}\) is already encoded by the
101 bits. The frozen record has no physical solver clock, independent
Home/horizon seal or independently defined release surface. A pair's own
inward recrossing cannot also be the external closure used to test its escape.

The open physical joins are therefore explicit:

- phase-to-seconds calibration;
- numerical carrier mass/energy weights;
- lossless T3 open-q to T4 closed-energy mapping;
- independently selected physical mirror and release surface;
- complete-event feedback acceptance;
- scheduler use of W9 history; and
- full RH-aware event semantics.

T6B carries exact dimensionless thermodynamic response through all 2,976
ledgers while preserving the carrier inventory and complete Theta18 address
multiset. It does not assign physical energy, temperature, duration, rate,
fusion threshold, numerical A or a physical mirror. Global SLC authority
remains frozen `SLCQ2-RZ`; bounded Starbreaker services retain their own typed
application lineage beneath that current/default selection surface.

## 8. Moving-information E/M1, SO1 and matter joins

### 8.1 E/M1 returning two-body relation

The isolated SB1-E/M1 successor compiles all 17,922 active Theta18 packets
into `384/384` elliptic two-body relations. Ordered electric and magnetic roles
carry the same exact C4 quarter turn as the Complete Write source: the state
returns while the relation retains the signed forward/reverse history needed
by later interference and receiver stages. **The test result suggests strong
contact with the concept.**

### 8.2 SO1 coherent spin-orbit wave

SB1-SO1 composes orbit, E-spin and M-spin signed waves before taking the Gram
across 2,048 synchronous source cases. All 2,048 pass. Component self Grams
remain planar rank two; coherent totals contain

```text
1,920 positive rank-two waves
  128 exact cancellations
    0 rank-one cases.
```

Each cross family is nonzero in 1,024 cases. The interference census is 896
constructive, 1,024 destructive and 128 completely cancelling. The nonzero
signed wave has period two while its shell-constant Gram has period one.
Unit, primary and independent checks pass `11/11`, `13/13` and `18/18`.
**The test result suggests strong contact with the concept.**

### 8.3 Live ATOM3D reception and re-emission

J1 executes the live Starbreaker source, E/M1 emissions and ATOM3D receivers
in one causal chain of 19,958 custody ledgers. J2 then uses the retained signed
off-fibre history to emit 1,536 ordered generation-two Theta18 packets and
extends custody to 20,345 rows. The same source compiled across all nine TDI3
relations yields an exact two-mode matter innovation positive for every real
`rho`. The receiver construction and controls are developed in
`SAMA-D000058`.

E/M1, SO1, J1 and J2 are isolated/non-promoted classical exact-information
successors. SO1 assigns no physical scale or numerical gravitational-wave
strain, and none changes a current pointer.

## 9. Established result and forward handoff

Starbreaker's exact formation object retains its native ledgers, atoms, stages,
orders, controls and readouts. Its structured execution closes the M126,
N144, N144+d and L162 accounting; its structure-first replay is byte-identical
and avoids dense fallback; and its W9 history remains an emit-only rank-161
receipt.

The application evidence is cumulative rather than selectively cleaned. The
external-collapse, typed-ledger, continuous-flow, pixel-morphology and
per-carrier surfaces each retain their declared boundary. Both failed-control
chains preserve the parent, freeze a narrow correction and replay the original
question without result-driven replacement.

The next application layer must supply the specific physical joins rather
than rename current dimensionless objects. No exact Starbreaker count or
source-contributor ledger by itself supplies strain, luminosity, seconds,
hertz, detector response or transported GW substance.

# Prime-Ruler and Mersenne Application

## Mersenne research: native validation and acquired policies — 14 September 2026

MP-GEN2-R4-V1 is installed on SLC-GEN3-R3. Native validation, bulk elimination and checkpoints are available. The recorded farm learns BULK_FIRST and reaches the same 156 terminals in 0.962856 s versus 2.077463 s. MP-B300 is owner-paused at 151 GPU batches, 148,914,569,216 proper factor lanes and 150 proof-learning cycles. The complete CSV deep pass contains 15,064 batches. Known-M1279 validation, factor-lane elimination and frontier candidate status retain their distinct meanings.

[Current derivations, code and results](../../research/mersenne/README.md).

## Retained source-era derivation and results

The following development retains its original experimental context and revision fields. Historical engine selections and campaign status in this source-era account are superseded by the dated current section above.


## Conceptual abstract

The Mersenne application begins from an owner-defined change of coordinate:
prime marks are informational radix coordinates on a closed two-dimensional
sphere, not equally spaced marks on an ordinary integer ruler. Exact prime
integers remain indispensable. They generate the marks, define operator
composition and carry every modular certificate. The conceptual change is in
the role assigned to a prime, not in its arithmetic identity.

The rational stereographic chart

\[
x=\tan(\chi/2),
\qquad
S(x)=\left(\frac{2x}{1+x^2},\frac{1-x^2}{1+x^2}\right)
\]

turns multiplication into an exact sphere action \(T_n(x)=nx\), inversion into
\(J(x)=1/x\), and logarithmic displacement into
\(du=dx/x=d\chi/\sin\chi\). Prime-only marks \(p^-\to p\) form reversible
adjacent cells. On each cell the program places three reciprocal voices:

\[
\alpha=2+\sqrt3,
\qquad
\beta_p=\frac{p+\sqrt3}{p-\sqrt3},
\qquad
\gamma_p=\beta_p\beta_{p^-}^{-1}.
\]

Repeated squaring generates dyadic pulses. A contact at depths \(i,j\) has
shallow depth \(s=\min(i,j)\) and delay \(d=|i-j|\). Transport into the shallow
frame produces an exact echo \(E\) satisfying

\[
E^{2^s}=1\pmod q,
\qquad
\operatorname{ord}_q(E)\mid2^s.
\]

The executed MP-S0--MP-S4B lineage compiles those objects into ECHO16 and
exposes the genuine Mersenne factor coordinate

\[
k=b2^e,
\qquad
q=2kp+1.
\]

A prime \(q\) is assigned as an exact factor of \(M_p=2^p-1\) only through the
order certificate \(\operatorname{ord}_q(2)=p\). MP-S5's sealed structural
\(k\)-order returns 103 factored objects against 100 for equal-budget ascending
counting, an installed strong-contact result in the application-scheduler
scope. MP-S6 assigns 1,282 exact factors among 5,390 exponents. MP-S7 and
MP-S8 preserve a 1,858-row roster after official-factor removal and complete
9,595-coordinate shell misses. Those misses remain
`PRIMALITY_UNASSIGNED`.

The clean-sheet route is a successor to two preserved failures. M4's 13,818
RH prime-sidecar ranking and M4A's 7,708 candidate-local Theta18/W9 ranking
both fail their fixed direct-successor questions. Their classifications remain
**The test falsifies the concept.** The clean-sheet compiler does not rename
those outcomes.

The owner has resumed only the bounded CEV1/MP successor and its initial
training. All other production, farm, frontier and publication work remains
paused. This chapter records the installed CEV1 and MPV2 successors without
launching arithmetic, remote work, promotion or distribution.

## 1. Opening question and conceptual picture

The governing question is:

> Can the prime-coordinate, reciprocal-history and dyadic structures be
> compiled into an exact ordering of genuine Mersenne factor coordinates,
> while keeping factor assignment, PRP status and Mersenne-primality
> certification strictly distinct?

The complete route is

```text
prime integer p and predecessor p^-
  -> prime-only reversible sphere cell
  -> alpha, beta_p and gamma_p reciprocal voices
  -> dyadic pulse depths and delayed echo
  -> structural k = b*2^e coordinate
  -> prime q = 2*k*p + 1
  -> exact modular order certificate
  -> factor assigned OR finite miss remains PRIMALITY_UNASSIGNED
```

This is an application compiler, not a universal replacement for the SLC
substrate, Q2 selector, Reciprocal History, RH authority or Lucas--Lehmer
certificate lane.

## 2. Informational radix with exact arithmetic custody

### 2.1 The owner-defined role

The owner interpretation is:

> Prime numbers are informational radix coordinates used to express orbit on
> a closed two-dimensional sphere.

The formalization retains two layers:

| Layer | Meaning |
|---|---|
| arithmetic encoding/certificate | \(p\) and \(q\) are exact certified prime integers; modular powers and divisibility are exact |
| SAM object role | each prime emits one indecomposable mark and multiplicative sphere action |

Uniform integer ordinal is not the ruler. Composite integers still appear
inside arithmetic values such as \(n\), \(k\) and (q-1); they simply do not emit
prime-ruler marks of their own.

### 2.2 Orbit and harmony are typed terms

`Orbit` denotes the discrete generated family

\[
z,z^2,z^4,\ldots,z^{2^n}
\]

on the exact information-state surface. It is not a claim of physical motion
around a sphere. `Harmony` denotes exact equality, reciprocal identity or
modular closure between separately generated voices. It is not an acoustic
analogy substituted for calculation.

## 3. Closed sphere and prime-only ruler

### 3.1 Rational sphere chart

Set \(x=\tan(\chi/2)\). The point

\[
S(x)=\left(X(x),Z(x)\right)
=\left(\frac{2x}{1+x^2},\frac{1-x^2}{1+x^2}\right)
\]

lies on the unit circle section of the closed two-dimensional sphere because

\[
X(x)^2+Z(x)^2
=\frac{4x^2+(1-x^2)^2}{(1+x^2)^2}
=\frac{1+2x^2+x^4}{(1+x^2)^2}
=1.
\]

The two limits \(x=0\) and \(x=\infty\) close the chart at reciprocal poles.

### 3.2 Multiplicative actions and reversal

Define

\[
T_n(x)=nx.
\]

Then operator composition is exactly integer multiplication:

\[
(T_m\circ T_n)(x)=m(nx)=(mn)x=T_{mn}(x).
\]

With \(J(x)=x^{-1}\),

\[
(J\circ T_n\circ J)(x)
=J\!\left(\frac n x\right)
=\frac x n
=T_{1/n}(x).
\]

Thus reversal conjugates expansion by \(n\) to contraction by \(1/n\).

Differentiating \(x=\tan(\chi/2)\) gives

\[
\frac{dx}{x}
=\frac{\tfrac12\sec^2(\chi/2)}{\tan(\chi/2)}d\chi
=\frac{d\chi}{2\sin(\chi/2)\cos(\chi/2)}
=\frac{d\chi}{\sin\chi}.
\]

Therefore

\[
du=\frac{dx}{x}=\frac{d\chi}{\sin\chi}.
\]

The logarithmic coordinate \(u=\log x\) converts \(T_n\) into translation by
\(\log n\), joining the sphere chart to the logarithmic translation language
of Reciprocal History without merging their application authorities.

### 3.3 Marks and adjacent cells

MP-S0B emits one object for each prime \(p\). A mark retains:

- the exact integer \(p\) and reciprocal point \(1/p\);
- indecomposable action \(T_p\);
- the two exact sphere points \(S(p)\) and \(S(1/p)\); and
- the adjacent arithmetic ports (p-1) and (p+1).

If \(p^-\) is the preceding prime, the directed adjacency \(p^-\to p\) defines
one reversible cell with scale

\[
\rho_p=\frac p{p^-},
\qquad
\rho_p^{-1}=\frac{p^-}{p},
\qquad
\Delta u_p=\log\frac p{p^-}.
\]

Its exact spherical chord is computed from the two rational sphere points.
MP-S0A/S0B install 172 prime-only marks and 171 adjacent-prime cells. No
composite between consecutive marks becomes an extra ruler object.

## 4. Three reciprocal voices

### 4.1 Native Lucas--Lehmer voice

Let

\[
\alpha=2+\sqrt3,
\qquad
\alpha^{-1}=2-\sqrt3,
\qquad
\alpha\alpha^{-1}=1.
\]

Modulo \(M_p=2^p-1\), define

\[
L_i=\alpha^{2^i}=a_i+b_i\sqrt3.
\]

The reciprocal trace is

\[
s_i=L_i+L_i^{-1}=2a_i.
\]

Because \(L_{i+1}=L_i^2\),

\[
s_{i+1}
=L_i^2+L_i^{-2}
=(L_i+L_i^{-1})^2-2
=s_i^2-2\pmod{M_p}.
\]

At \(i=0\),

\[
s_0=\alpha+\alpha^{-1}=4,
\]

so the common reciprocal mode is exactly the Lucas--Lehmer recurrence. MP-S0
reconstructs this mode for all 171 prime exponents through 1024.

### 4.2 Candidate prime-mark voice

The Cayley lift

\[
\beta_p=\frac{p+\sqrt3}{p-\sqrt3}
\]

has reciprocal conjugate

\[
\beta_p^{-1}=\frac{p-\sqrt3}{p+\sqrt3}.
\]

Its norm is one, so its dyadic family

\[
R_j=\beta_p^{2^j}
\]

lives on the same reciprocal quadratic-unit surface as the \(\alpha\) family.
The prime integer selects the voice; the modular and quadratic arithmetic
retains exact custody.

### 4.3 Incoming transition beat

The transition from the previous prime cell is

\[
\gamma_p=\beta_p\beta_{p^-}^{-1}.
\]

This object records change between consecutive prime voices rather than a
second copy of the current mark. Its inverse reverses the cell:

\[
\gamma_p^{-1}=\beta_{p^-}\beta_p^{-1}.
\]

The three families answer different questions: \(\alpha\) carries the native
Mersenne recurrence, \(\beta_p\) carries the current prime mark and
\(\gamma_p\) carries the adjacent-prime transition.

## 5. Beats within beats

### 5.1 Dyadic depth and delay

Repeated squaring of any voice produces pulses at depths

\[
z^{2^0},z^{2^1},\ldots,z^{2^n}.
\]

For a contact between depth \(i\) in one voice and depth \(j\) in another, set

\[
s=\min(i,j),
\qquad
d=|i-j|.
\]

If, for example, \(j=i+d\), then

\[
z^{2^j}=\left(z^{2^i}\right)^{2^d}.
\]

The exponent \(2^d\) transports the deeper pulse into the shallower frame. The
residual relation is the base echo \(E\). Every retained MP-S2 and MP-S3 factor
component satisfies

\[
E^{2^s}=1\pmod q,
\qquad
\operatorname{ord}_q(E)\mid2^s.
\]

The echo is therefore an exact finite-order modular object with a dyadic
closure depth, not a visual correlation.

### 5.2 Nested compiler

Factor the Mersenne multiplier coordinate as

\[
k=b2^e,
\]

where \(b\) is odd and \(e=v_2(k)\). The structural base \(b\) and dyadic scale
\(2^e\) echo the prime-cell and pulse decomposition. The complete compiled
route is

```text
adjacent-prime cell
  -> alpha/beta/gamma reciprocal relation
  -> shallow depth s and echo delay d
  -> base echo closure
  -> odd structural base b and dyadic scale 2^e
  -> k = b*2^e
  -> q = 2*k*p + 1
```

This is the exact content of `beats within beats`: nested coordinate
relations are compiled into a candidate factor address that the modular lane
can certify or reject.

## 6. Exact Mersenne factor certificate

### 6.1 Deriving the candidate form

Let \(p\) be prime and suppose prime \(q\) divides

\[
M_p=2^p-1.
\]

Then

\[
2^p\equiv1\pmod q.
\]

The multiplicative order \(r=\operatorname{ord}_q(2)\) divides \(p\). For a
nontrivial Mersenne factor, \(r\ne1\); since \(p\) is prime, \(r=p\). Fermat's
theorem also gives \(r\mid q-1\), hence

\[
p\mid q-1.
\]

Because \(q\) is odd, (q-1) is even, so for some positive integer \(k\),

\[
q-1=2kp,
\qquad
q=2kp+1.
\]

Conversely, if the candidate \(q=2kp+1\) is prime and the exact order check
returns

\[
\operatorname{ord}_q(2)=p,
\]

then \(2^p\equiv1\pmod q\) and therefore \(q\mid M_p\). The implementation may
certify the modular closure with `pow(2,p,q)=1` together with the declared
prime/order conditions. It never needs to materialize the enormous integer
\(2^p-1\).

# Jacobian Program and Clean Restart

## SLC-GEN3-R4: current computation — 16 September 2026

SLC-GEN3-R4 and SLC-GEN3-CEV1-R4 are the sole current global runtime and CE, generation GEN3-SOURCEOPERATORS1-20260916-G1 (H001469). Complete exact histories and logarithmic accounts now include certified scalar/spectral logs and eight source-operator operations. Source-operator qualification records 50 candidate and 157 installed-adoption checks. The public portable package retains its September10 R3 binding; the current R4 capability sources and research records are a separate supplement. GEN3-RXT-R7.1 retains its September14 deployment context.

[Current derivations, code and results](../../docs/ARCHITECTURE.md).

## Retained source-era derivation and results

The following development retains its original experimental context and revision fields. Historical engine selections and campaign status in this source-era account are superseded by the dated current section above.


## Conceptual abstract

The Jacobian program asks how an exact polynomial map can return its target
state while retaining a nontrivial history of sheets, braids, escape and
projective completion. That is a mathematical and computational question, so
JC1 and HF1--HF6TH belong in Volume III. Their earlier matter-volume-labelled files
remain immutable provenance, but relocation changes placement and current
interpretation rather than any executed result.

JC1 begins with the exact polynomial map

\[
\begin{aligned}
P(x,y,z)&=(1+xy)^3z+y^2(1+xy)(4+3xy),\\
Q(x,y,z)&=y+3x(1+xy)^2z+3xy^2(4+3xy),\\
R(x,y,z)&=2x-3x^2y-x^3z,
\end{aligned}
\]

whose Jacobian determinant simplifies identically to

\[
\det D(P,Q,R)=-2.
\]

An exact elimination chart produces a cubic fiber polynomial, its resultant
and discriminant. On the frozen slice \(V=-16/27,R=1\), one winding around
\(U=0\) transposes two escaping sheets, while double winding returns the sheet
permutation to the identity. The same winding programs compile through
Complete Exact Write on one Theta18 axis. Four positive writes, four negative
writes and eight positive writes all have zero compiled translation across all
262,144 addresses, yet their ordered W9 receipts retain four, four and eight
events with different orientations and semantic hashes. JC1 passes 18/18;
independent validation passes 9/9. Its exact classification is **The test result suggests strong contact with the concept.**

The portable result is narrower and stronger than an endpoint slogan:
endpoint equality does not imply program-history equality. It does not by
itself install JC1 monodromy, a quotient, a Newton field or a tensor/path-jet
field as a matter payload.

HF1--HF6TH then form a valuable but historically scaffolded discovery chain:
local reducers and a chosen `Z4^2` cell grammar; nonproper meridians and exact
polynomial cells; finite versus projective sheet custody; path-dependent
escape corrections; blinded path-jet discriminators; fresh-map transfer;
Newton-polygon signatures; and a complete mixed-jet compiler. Every executed
finding and correction remains in custody. The current clean restart does not
assume that the HF2 torus, its generators, `H_SAM`, W9 or any carrier/matter
meaning arises naturally from the polynomial map.

The restart therefore reverses the order of dependence: reconstruct the
source algebra independently, derive invariants from actual fibers and the
regular-value set, freeze algebraically generated loops, examine monodromy,
properness and infinity, and only then reveal legacy HF observations. An
optional SAM comparison requires an explicit compiler. A later matter application
requires carrier compilation, reciprocal preservation, an identified matter
consumer and a load-bearing controlled output change. None has executed. This
future bridge is not standalone SAMA Volume IV, which is the RH program.

## 2. Exact JC1 source algebra

### 2.1 Polynomial map and constant Jacobian

Let

\[
F:\mathbb A^3\to\mathbb A^3,
\qquad
F(x,y,z)=(P,Q,R)
\]

with \(P,Q,R\) as stated above. The derivative matrix is formed by differentiating
each component with respect to ((x,y,z)). Exact polynomial expansion and
factorization of its determinant cancel every variable-dependent term and
leave

\[
\det DF=-2.
\]

The determinant is nonzero at every affine source point. Consequently the
affine map has no local Jacobian singularity. This does not imply properness:
source branches may still escape to infinity while their target values remain
finite.

### 2.2 Elimination chart

JC1 uses

\[
x=\frac1m,
\qquad
y=m(s-1),
\qquad
z=(5-3s-m)m^2.
\]

Substitution into \(P,Q,R\), followed by elimination of \(m\), gives the exact
resultant

\[
\operatorname{Res}_m(U-Q,V-P)
=s^3\Phi(U,V;s),
\]

where

\[
\Phi(U,V;s)=K(U,V)s^3+(U^2-12V)s-4V
\]

and

\[
K(U,V)=U^3-U^2-18UV+27V^2+16V.
\]

The cubic discriminant factors as

\[
\operatorname{Disc}_s\Phi
=-4\bigl(U^3-18UV+54V^2\bigr)^2K(U,V).
\]

Thus the two load-bearing target components are

\[
D(U,V)=U^3-18UV+54V^2
\]

and \(K(U,V)\). Their repeated and simple factors distinguish finite-root braid
behavior from coefficient loss and projective escape.

### 2.3 Exact calibration and loss fibers

The target \((P,Q,R)=(-1,1,-1)\) has the exact eliminated \(x\)-fiber

\[
(x-1)(3x+1)(3x+2),
\]

with three exact sources

\[
(1,-2,9),
\quad
(-1/3,4,27),
\quad
(-2/3,-1/2,-9/8).
\]

Each maps to the same target and has Jacobian \(-2\).

At the coefficient-loss target

\[
(U,V,R)=(0,-16/27,1),
\]

the unique finite source is

\[
(x,y,z)=(1/2,-8/3,16).
\]

The eliminated \(x\)-fiber drops to \((2x-1)/2\). Two other cubic branches have
moved to projective infinity rather than collided at an affine Jacobian zero.

## 3. Monodromy from the fiber

### 3.1 Frozen slice

On \(V=-16/27\),

\[
K(U,-16/27)=\frac{U(3U^2-3U+32)}3.
\]

At \(U=0\), the cubic coefficient vanishes while

\[
\Phi(0,-16/27;s)
=\frac{64}{9}s+\frac{64}{27}.
\]

One branch therefore tends to \(s=-1/3\). Balancing the cubic and linear terms
for the other two branches gives

\[
s^2U\longrightarrow-\frac23.
\]

Those branches escape with magnitude proportional to \(|U|^{-1/2}\).

### 3.2 Loop continuation

Take a small circle

\[
U(\theta)=\rho e^{i\theta},
\qquad
V=-16/27,
\qquad
R=1.
\]

At each step, numerically continue the three roots of \(\Phi\) by minimum
matching to the preceding roots. A positive winding and a negative winding
both return the finite branch and exchange the two escaping branches:

\[
[0,1,2]\longmapsto[0,2,1].
\]

The opposite loop orientation changes path direction but not the
transposition. A double winding applies the transposition twice:

\[
(1\ 2)^2=e,
\]

so the sheet permutation returns to `[0,1,2]`. The offset zero-winding control
also returns identity. Maximum recorded fiber residuals stay below
\(5\times10^{-13}\) on the winding paths.

This chain separates target return, sheet permutation and path winding. Double
winding can have identity permutation while still containing a nonempty
ordered program.

## 4. Exact Write compilation and retained history

### 4.1 Theta18 event map

Theta18 addresses form \(\mathbb Z_4^9\) in packed two-bit coordinates. On axis
\(j\), one positive or negative event applies the exact signed \(\mathbb Z_4\)
step. For axis zero and initial address zero, the positive program visits

```text
0 -> 1 -> 512 -> 513 -> 0
```

while the negative program visits

```text
0 -> 513 -> 512 -> 1 -> 0.
```

Four equal signed steps are zero modulo four. Hence the programs

\[
w_+=(0,+1)^4,
\qquad
w_-=(0,-1)^4,
\qquad
w_{++}=(0,+1)^8
\]

all compile to translation zero. Because the compiled action is a group
translation, zero translation returns every one of the 262,144 input
addresses, not only address zero. Sequential and compiled native execution
agree on the full address set.

### 4.2 Ordered receipt construction

Before endpoint collapse, retain for event \(r\)

\[
(j_r,\epsilon_r,a_{r-1},a_r).
\]

The JC1-to-W9 adapter assigns event \(r\) to reciprocal channel \(r\), with

\[
L_r=\epsilon_r,
\qquad
R_r=-\epsilon_r.
\]

The W9 common and directional components are

\[
C_r=L_r+R_r=0,
\qquad
A_r=L_r-R_r=2\epsilon_r.
\]

Therefore the positive four-event receipt begins
((2,2,2,2)), the negative receipt begins ((-2,-2,-2,-2)), and the double
receipt has eight positive entries. Their \(L^1\) receipt norms are 8, 8 and
16, and their semantic hashes are distinct. Reciprocal reconstruction is

\[
L_r=\frac{C_r+A_r}{2},
\qquad
R_r=\frac{C_r-A_r}{2}.
\]

The exact endpoint is the same; event count, sign, order and receipt remain
different.

### 4.3 JC1 result and boundary

The principal campaign passes 18/18 and independent validation passes 9/9.
The result is classified:

**The test result suggests strong contact with the concept.**

The established portable statement is:

```text
endpoint compilation can erase which closed program occurred;
an ordered event receipt can retain that distinction.
```

This directly joins the append-only reel of `SAMA-D000042`. It does not imply
that a particular carrier, intersection or matter ledger consumes the full
JC1 polynomial object.

## 5. Preserved HF1--HF6TH lineage

### 5.1 HF1: reducer foundation

HF1 separates endpoint action in \(\mathbb Z_4^9\) from an unwrapped signed lift
in \(\mathbb Z^9\). For an endpoint-returning word \(w\),

\[
\omega(w)=\widetilde z(w)/4\in\mathbb Z^9.
\]

It reduces only adjacent exact reciprocal writes with matching route,
payload, account anchor and completion type. Four same-orientation writes
retain a completed turn. Generic freeze passes 42/42, JC1 calibration 41/41
and independent reconstruction 54/54. A first 41/42 receipt is preserved:
its validator mistook boundary prose for embedded map coefficients. The
corrected validator checks actual coefficient markers. HF1's classification
is **The test result suggests strong contact with the concept.**

### 5.2 HF2: chosen local `Z4^2` grammar

HF2 installs reciprocal bigons and signed commuting squares on a chosen
(`x`,`y`) `Z4^2` surface. Termination yields normal form

\[
x^my^n,
\]

while endpoint compilation retains only \((m\bmod4,n\bmod4)\). The resulting
history quotient is

\[
H_{local}\cong\mathbb Z^2.
\]

Among 87,381 words through length eight, 8,909 return the endpoint, 5,341 are
locally null and 3,568 retain nonzero history. This falsifies the fixed target
that every endpoint-returning word in that installed grammar is locally
exact-null. The result does not establish that the chosen torus or its two
generators are natural invariants of JC1.

### 5.3 HF3/HF4: nonproper obstruction and exact regular cell

HF3 follows the (K/x) meridian. Its radial filling reaches coefficient loss:
one source stays finite while two escape with exponent \(-1/2\). Positive,
negative and double filling attempts remain `NONPROPER_OBSTRUCTION`; no exact
cell is promoted. HF3 passes 28/28, separated promotion 24/24 and independent
reconstruction 46/46, with strong contact at the obstruction-localization
scope.

HF4 shows the obstruction is not a fixed-slice artifact by constructing the
full nonproper hypersurface and its nonzero meridian linking. Separately, a
(D/y) meridian has identity sheet permutation, pure-braid winding one and no
escape. An exact Gaussian-rational source disk certifies boundary ((0,1)), so

\[
\Lambda_{JC1}^{certified}=\langle(0,1)\rangle,
\qquad
\mathbb Z^2/\Lambda_{JC1}^{certified}\cong\mathbb Z.
\]

Two implementation attempts are preserved: NumPy-boolean serialization and a
closed-path final-sample tracking defect. Corrected execution passes 37/37;
promotion and independent reconstructions pass their declared gates. The
result suggests strong contact.

### 5.4 HF5 and HF5A: selected-sheet fill versus global sheet custody

HF5 constructs an exact source disk with \(K\)-winding one and certifies a
regular finite sheet through the coefficient-loss center. Its first
interpretation promoted ((1,0)), combined it with ((0,1)) and concluded a full
lattice. H000195/H000196 preserve that literal bridge route and its exact
calculations.

HF5A corrects the current interpretation: the finite-sheet disk does not fill
all three typed sheets. Homogenizing

\[
\Phi^h=KS^3+BST^2-4VT^3
\]

gives at projective infinity

\[
\Phi^h|_{T=0}=KS^3.
\]

At the loss center the local infinity equation is

\[
\frac{64}{27}t^2(t+3)=0.
\]

One finite root survives and a double root remains at infinity. The response
\(I_\infty(k,d)=k\) retains the (K/x) global-history class. Current preserved HF
authority therefore returns to the rank-one certified lattice above. HF5A
passes 26/26 with 22/22 independent reconstruction and suggests strong
contact. Three pre-result field/dependency/alias faults remain preserved.

### 5.5 HF6 and the path-specific correction

HF6 emits anonymous finite-plus-projective receipts before attaching sector
labels. It reconstructs:

| Sector | finite/infinity sheets | infinity index | permutation | custody |
|---|---:|---:|---|---|
| `D/y` | 3/0 | 0 | identity | `GLOBALLY_DISCHARGED` |
| `K/x` | 1/2 | 1 | `[0,2,1]` | `MIXED` |

Seven nonslice deformations preserve \(K\)-winding one, the transposition and
the surviving class. Execution passes 19/19 and independent validation 24/24;
the result suggests strong contact.

H000199 corrects one overgeneralized coefficient. The escape exponent remains
\(-1/2\), but the leading coefficient depends on the exact path jet. The radial
route alone has \(s^2U\to-2/3\); six other slopes and the HF5 disk have their own
exact values. The correction passes 15/15 and 14/14 and does not change HF6's
classification or quotient.

### 5.6 HF6T/HF6TF: blinded jet rule and transfer boundary

HF6T freezes the eliminant-conditioned rule

```text
a3(0)=0 and ord_path(a3)=1 and b1(0)!=0
  -> PROJECTIVE_CUSTODY_CANDIDATE
otherwise
  -> FINITE_OR_UNRESOLVED
```

before reveal. It matches nine forward routes and nine reversals, reconstructs
path-specific escape and preserves the failed pure-tensor partition. A first
18/19 run used the wrong determinant sign after swapping target coordinate
order; correcting only that explicit sign gives 19/19. The result suggests
strong contact for the JC1 path-jet discriminator, not a universal tensor law.

HF6TF freezes the rule unchanged on ten fresh maps. It returns four true
positives, zero false positives, two false negatives and four true negatives.
It is exact 7/7 on the admitted proper-cubic/simple-transverse domain, but it
misses quadratic coefficient loss and simultaneous cubic/linear degeneration.
Those misses remain evidence and motivate the next signature rather than a
retroactive threshold change.

### 5.7 HF6TG/HF6TH: Newton signature and mixed-jet compiler

For

\[
\Phi_t(s)=\sum_j a_j(t)s^j,
\qquad
\nu_j=\operatorname{ord}_t a_j(t),
\]

HF6TG takes the lower convex hull of \((j,\nu_j)\). Positive-slope edge width,
slope and initial polynomial predict escaping-sheet count, exponent and
regular cycle structure. On 15 fresh fibers, projective presence, sheet counts
and coefficient winding close 15/15; 34/34 exponents and 10/10 regular cycle
types reconstruct. A repeated edge remains `SINGULARITY_OBSTRUCTION`. The
result suggests strong contact without installing a universal Newton law.

HF6TH then works in the triangular affine-path chart and freezes the complete
mixed coefficient

\[
\mathcal T_{rj}
=\frac1{r!j!}D^{r+j}F^2(v^r,w^j).
\]

For

\[
G(x,y)=\sum_{p,j}c_{pj}x^py^j,
\]

exact Taylor expansion gives

\[
\mathcal T_{rj}
=\sum_{p\ge r}c_{pj}\binom pr u_0^{p-r},
\qquad
\nu_j=\min\{r:\mathcal T_{rj}\ne0\}.
\]

The complete mixed jet therefore reconstructs the Newton signature in that
chart. Known calibration closes 15/15; twelve fresh maps reconstruct all
valuation tables, hulls, edge signatures and projective counts, with 40/40
escape exponents. This is a chart-specific exact compiler identity. Low-order
compression, nontriangular maps, nonaffine paths and coordinate-invariant
transfer remain open.

## 6. What the legacy lineage does and does not install

The HF lineage contains exact reducers, cells, obstructions, projective
receipts, blind-transfer results and compiler identities. Its stages remain
valid in their declared charts. The current program makes three distinctions:

1. the HF2 grammar is a constructed SLC object, not yet a source-derived JC1
   invariant;
2. projective and mixed-jet findings do not constitute a universal tensor law;
3. historically matter-volume-labelled carrier supplements are preserved route
   artifacts, not current matter interfaces.

No current JC1-specific history, sheet, quotient, Newton or tensor field is
consumed by AFC2--AFC4. No `q_A`, mass, SI energy or matter-generation relation
is installed.

relation, composition law, base-point and coordinate dependence, loop-reversal
and concatenation behavior, ordinary invertible-map value and JC1 value or

The legacy reveal occurs only after the clean source, definitions and first
predictions are sealed. Agreement, disagreement, partial rediscovery and
irrelevance are all retained. This prevents legacy success from becoming a
hidden premise.

## 8. Future matter-bridge boundary

A future Jacobian object reaches a matter application only if one bridge-native campaign
establishes all four joins:

1. an exact compiler from the source-derived invariant into a declared carrier
   payload;
2. exact preservation of that payload through reciprocal delivery;
3. an identified intersection or matter-ledger consumer that reads it; and
4. a load-bearing output change when the payload is altered under a controlled
   comparison.

The first two establish transport; the third establishes consumption; the
fourth establishes consequence. A history hash that survives delivery without
a consumer is not the completed bridge. A consumer that changes for an
undeclared correlated field is not the controlled payload result.

No current JC1/HF field has completed this chain. Depth training, Q2 selection,
W9 availability or A-field-carrier existence alone does not validate or
promote a Jacobian-to-matter interpretation.

The source program called this a prospective `Volume IV` boundary. That exact
wording remains in the immutable source and `SAMA-C000256-R001`. In this
standalone atlas, Volume IV is the Riemann Hypothesis program. The matter bridge
therefore remains unnumbered, unexecuted and outside the Volume IV dependency
graph unless a later source-bound RH relation is separately established.

The next mathematical step is the unexecuted source-derived restart. Its first

# Nonzero Amplification — The Quantum-to-Galactic Bridge

## SLC-GEN3-R4: current computation — 16 September 2026

SLC-GEN3-R4 and SLC-GEN3-CEV1-R4 are the sole current global runtime and CE, generation GEN3-SOURCEOPERATORS1-20260916-G1 (H001469). Complete exact histories and logarithmic accounts now include certified scalar/spectral logs and eight source-operator operations. Source-operator qualification records 50 candidate and 157 installed-adoption checks. The public portable package retains its September10 R3 binding; the current R4 capability sources and research records are a separate supplement. GEN3-RXT-R7.1 retains its September14 deployment context.

[Current derivations, code and results](../../docs/ARCHITECTURE.md).

## Retained source-era derivation and results

The following development retains its original experimental context and revision fields. Historical engine selections and campaign status in this source-era account are superseded by the dated current section above.


## 1. Orientation

SAM already contains three ingredients that are usually discussed at
different scales: a nonzero accumulation field sourced by matter, an exact
W8/X1/W9 write lifecycle with Theta carrier structure, and the accumulated
effect of many stellar sources in galaxy halos. The Nonzero Amplification
Principle identifies these as one recursive mechanism.

The central statement is:

> **Matter writes. The substrate carries. Nonzero writes accumulate.**

Matter does not have to occupy the route between two sources for information
to cross that route. A source changes the substrate accumulation; the lifted
substrate carries the source-conditioned write through matter-free space. The
same composition that turns particle-scale writes into a body-scale presence
can turn stellar writes into a galactic accumulation halo.

This document records an owner-originated conceptual bridge for continued
mathematical development. It does not assign a test result classification.

## 2. Established inputs and new principle

### 2.1 Established inputs

The new bridge begins from established, separately typed SAM structures:

- the universal accumulation floor `A_0`;
- the spherical source accumulation lift `A(r)`;
- the closed-matter return to accumulation through source support and carrier
  compression;
- the W8/X1/W9 substrate-atom relation;
- Theta18 as a tensor carrier; and
- the Complete Exact Write computation surface.

These inputs retain their established definitions. The new concept does not
replace `A_0`, redefine W9 as a separate object, turn Theta18 into matter, or
replace the Exact Write with a continuum metaphor.

### 2.2 Owner-originated Nonzero Amplification Principle

> **Every nonzero constituent substrate write survives coherent composition,
> allowing particle-scale information to accumulate recursively into
> macroscopic and galactic accumulation structure.**

The principle adds a cross-scale interpretation to the established inputs:

1. a particle contributes a nonzero source-conditioned write;
2. many particle writes compose into the coherent write of a body;
3. the body-scale write is carried by matter-free substrate;
4. overlapping body writes establish local informational contact;
5. body writes compose again at larger scales; and
6. billions of stellar writes form the extended galactic accumulation halo.

## 3. The information-bearing substrate

Introduce provisional notation for the source-conditioned lift:

\[
\Delta A_S(x)=A_{\mathrm{total},S}(x)-A_0.
\]

This notation distinguishes three states that must not be collapsed:

| State | Meaning |
|---|---|
| Matter present | A local material source participates in creating or changing the write. |
| Matter absent and `Delta A_S(x)>0` | The substrate remains source-conditioned and carries information without an intervening material messenger. |
| `Delta A_S(x)=0`, equivalently the source route has returned to `A=A_0` | The active source-conditioned connection is absent at that point. |

The substrate is therefore not a passive geometric backdrop. Changes in `A`
change the local write environment. In the owner-originated mechanism, those
changes continually affect the W8/X1/W9 lifecycle and Theta carrier activity
throughout the connected matter-free region.

Define the source-conditioned contact domain provisionally by

\[
\Omega_S=\{x:\Delta A_S(x)>0\}.
\]

A route between two systems is in active source contact when it remains in
the appropriate connected component of this lifted domain. A point at which
the source-conditioned lift returns to zero supplies the proposed
disconnection condition.

## 4. Exact composition rather than point-particle messaging

For a composite source `S`, write

\[
\Delta A_S(x)
=
\mathcal C_S\!\left(\{\delta A_i(x):i\in S\}\right),
\]

where each constituent contribution is nonzero on its active route and
`mathcal C_S` denotes the exact composition law.

The symbol `mathcal C_S` is deliberately not replaced by ordinary linear
addition. SAM has an Exact Write, retained histories, typed carriers and
directional computation. The correct composition law should be recovered or
derived from that algebra so that cross terms, history and carrier structure
remain intact.

The conceptual content is nevertheless exact enough to distinguish the
mechanisms:

- an individual Earth particle does not send an isolated command to an
  individual Moon particle;
- all particle writes in Earth compose into an Earth-scale accumulated write;
- all particle writes in the Moon compose into a Moon-scale accumulated
  write; and
- their source-conditioned domains overlap in the matter-free substrate,
  where continuing changes in `A`, W8/X1/W9 and Theta provide local contact.

The Moon responds inside the Earth's accumulated substrate write while also
contributing its own write to the same substrate.

## 5. One recursive bridge across three scales

### 5.1 Quantum to macroscopic

The quantum bridge is the passage from exact local substrate transactions to
a coherent composite source:

\[
\{\text{particle Exact Writes}\}
\xrightarrow{\mathcal C}
\text{body-scale accumulated write}.
\]

Object-level informational presence need not be introduced as an unrelated
primitive if it is the coherent closure of the constituent writes.

### 5.2 Earth to Moon

Earth and Moon participate in one shared lifted substrate. Their continuing
internal particle activity changes their composite writes; those changes are
carried through matter-free space by the substrate itself. The orbital
response is therefore downstream of an information-bearing write process,
not merely a description of the resulting path.

### 5.3 Stars to galaxy

The galactic bridge applies the same composition recursively:

\[
\{\text{stellar body writes}\}
\xrightarrow{\mathcal C}
\text{galaxy-scale accumulated halo}.
\]

Each star contributes a nonzero effect at large distance. Billions of such
contributions produce the extended galactic accumulation structure. The
particle-to-body and star-to-galaxy steps are thus two applications of the
same nonzero-preserving composition law.

## 6. Technical continuation

The next investigation begins with the exact algebra already present in SAM:

1. **Composition operator.** Identify `mathcal C` inside Complete Exact Write
   and SLC execution, including retained-history and directional terms.
2. **Nonzero preservation.** Determine the exact condition under which a
   constituent nonzero write survives composite closure.
3. **A-dependent lifecycle.** State the operator by which a local change in
   `A` changes W8/X1/W9 execution and Theta carrier activity.
4. **Contact domain.** Derive the connected `A>A_0` route and the exact
   `A=A_0` disconnection condition.
5. **Recursive closure.** Determine when a composite write is admitted as a
   source input to the same operation at the next scale.
6. **Existing SLC orbit.** Inspect whether the current particle-orbit emulator
   already contains part of this composition and transport law.
7. **Cross-scale comparison.** Express particle/body, Earth--Moon and
   star/galaxy examples through one shared receipt format.

This route is intended to discover the exact operator already implied by the
combined SAM structures, rather than introduce separate quantum, orbital and
galactic mechanisms.

## 7. Document connections

galactic accumulation application from `SAMA-D000022`, and the exact
transaction machinery from `SAMA-D000038` through `SAMA-D000040`. Its future
successors should return the recovered composition operator to those focused
documents without rewriting their established definitions.

# Relational Nonzero Amplification and Interaction Closure

## SLC-GEN3-R4: current computation — 16 September 2026

SLC-GEN3-R4 and SLC-GEN3-CEV1-R4 are the sole current global runtime and CE, generation GEN3-SOURCEOPERATORS1-20260916-G1 (H001469). Complete exact histories and logarithmic accounts now include certified scalar/spectral logs and eight source-operator operations. Source-operator qualification records 50 candidate and 157 installed-adoption checks. ## Retained source-era derivation and results

The following development retains its original experimental context and revision fields. Historical engine selections and campaign status in this source-era account are superseded by the dated current section above.


## 1. Orientation

The first Nonzero Amplification document establishes that constituent
substrate writes can compose recursively from particles to bodies and from
stellar bodies to galaxies. This document adds the load-bearing correction:
composition depends on how the constituents interact.

Two planets light-years apart and the same two planets closely orbiting have
the same constituent inventory. They do not have the same reciprocal history,
overlap, directional relation or completed substrate write. The nearby
orbiting pair forms a coherent composite source; the distant pair approaches
two separately carried source writes.

The central statement is:

> **Nonzero amplification is relational. Interacting writes create completed
> information that is absent from an inventory-only sum.**

## 2. Inventory and interaction are differently typed

Let `S` be a composite system. Its source data includes at least:

- constituent writes `{w_i}`;
- a typed active relation network `R_S`;
- retained reciprocal or directional history `H_S`;
- the local and route-dependent accumulation state; and
- the completed W8/X1/W9 and Theta carrier state.

The proposed completed composition is therefore written schematically as

\[
\Delta A_S
=
\mathcal C_S\!\left(\{w_i\},R_S,H_S\right).
\]

An inventory-only control would instead evaluate

\[
\Delta A_{\mathrm{diag}}=\sum_i\delta A_i.
\]

The difference

\[
\mathcal I_S
=
\Delta A_S-\Delta A_{\mathrm{diag}}
\]

is a useful diagnostic representation of the interaction innovation. It does
not define `mathcal C_S` as ordinary addition. The native composition must
retain Exact Write types, histories, cross terms and completion.

## 3. Separation changes the completed write

For the same two constituents, consider two relational states.

### 3.1 Distant state

The two source-conditioned writes are carried separately. Their active
overlap and continuing reciprocal history are reduced, and a route returning
to the universal floor supplies the proposed disconnection condition.

### 3.2 Nearby orbiting state

Each source continually changes the accumulation environment of the other.
Those changes alter the matter-free substrate between them, the local
W8/X1/W9 lifecycle and Theta carrier/intersection activity. The pair therefore
generates a continuing relational history and becomes a new composite source.

The distinction is not “two sources versus more sources.” It is:

\[
(w_1,w_2,R_{\mathrm{distant}},H_{\mathrm{distant}})
\neq
(w_1,w_2,R_{\mathrm{orbit}},H_{\mathrm{orbit}}).
\]

## 4. Many-body amplification

For many particles, the number of possible relations grows faster than the
constituent count. Pair, cycle, face and higher closure information can
therefore become load-bearing even when every individual particle write is
small.

A schematic cluster display is

\[
\Delta A_S
\sim
\sum_i A_i
+\sum_{i<j}\mathcal I_{ij}
+\sum_{i<j<k}\mathcal I_{ijk}
+\cdots,
\]

but the Exact Write composition owns the actual algebra. The display only
shows what a spreadsheet-style constituent sum omits: interaction terms whose
values depend on the completed relational state.

This gives Nonzero Amplification two inseparable aspects:

1. each constituent contributes a nonzero write; and
2. coherent interaction among those writes generates additional completed
   information.

## 5. ATOM3D consequence

ATOM3D has frequently represented candidate assemblies through counts,
independent relation rows and separately scored receipt features. The current
Li-6 action-frame frontier exposes the limit of that representation: complete
W9P custody retains each relation but supplies no relation-to-frame identity,
leaving 13,824 reciprocal frame assignments.

Relational Nonzero Amplification changes the unit evaluated by a successor.
The unit should be the whole active relation network, with each relation read
in the context of all co-active writes:

\[
(e,f_e,R_S,H_S,\Delta A_S,\Theta_S)
\longmapsto
\mathcal I_e(S).
\]

Here `e` is a relation, `f_e` its action frame and `mathcal I_e(S)` its
context-dependent innovation inside the completed assembly. The same isolated
edge placed in a different network need not produce the same innovation.

The direct successor is therefore an **interaction-aware composite-write
receipt**, not another isolated edge selector. Candidate Li-6 assemblies can
then be compared by exact completed closure while preserving the current bars
against geometry fitting and opaque selection.

## 6. RH consequence

The corresponding RH distinction is between eventwise inventory and completed
coupling. If `W` is assembled from event writes `W_i`, then

\[
W^*W
=
\sum_i W_i^*W_i
+\sum_{i\ne j}W_i^*W_j.
\]

The off-diagonal products carry relational information. Current RH work
already retains completed Gamma--prime--Robin coupling where eventwise
positivity is insufficient, and its support extensions are expressed through
transported carriers and entrant Schur innovations.

Relational Nonzero Amplification supplies a shared candidate origin for those
cross terms: they are the mathematical receipt of coexisting source writes
interacting inside one completed substrate history.

## 7. Technical continuation

1. Recover `mathcal C_S` from Complete Exact Write and current SLC receipts.
2. Compile whole-network interaction innovations without reducing relations
   to independent rows.
3. Compare identical inventories under distant, nearby, orbiting and
   relation-deleted configurations.
4. Determine which interaction data selects or reduces ATOM3D action frames.
5. Express the resulting operator as a completed Gram or Schur innovation.
6. Cross-walk that operator to the RH source-append architecture.
